- **efficient-expression-create**: 把已经确认的内容方向、目标受众和真实素材创作成完整的短视频或口播稿。按照“话题全貌 → 人群与主张 → 大结构 → 小结构 → 节奏 → 字词与印象组合 → 必要转化”的顺序生产内容，并交付结构图、成稿和事实来源审计。用户说“方向定了帮我写稿”“把这些经历组织成一条内容”“从素材写完整口播”“不要只给框架”时使用。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-create))
- **efficient-expression-direction**: 为短视频、口播和内容营销确定正确的内容方向。展开话题全貌，区分主题与选题，比较不同人群、处境、利益位置和立场，选择核心人群、主张、关键词与可讲/不宜讲角度。用户说“这个选题能不能做”“该对谁讲”“应该站什么角度”“内容方向怎么定”“只有一个主题不知道讲什么”时使用。只交付方向与话题地图，不直接代写完整稿。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-direction))
- **efficient-expression-discovery**: 用“三张纸”梳理 IP 或创始人的资源经历、理想商业模式、行业机会、最佳生态终局、最佳增长起点、TOP 用户画像，以及“体系/人物/产品/案例 × 总/分”的初步转化内容。用户说“先把我的业务梳理清楚”“账号不知道讲什么”“整理客户画像和产品”“做项目尽调”“盘一下人产案”时使用，也适用于读取访谈逐字稿、DOCX、Markdown 或现有业务材料后生成项目发现简报。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-discovery))
- **efficient-expression-first-screen**: 组合优化内容最前面的三个观看判断点：标题、描述内容和首句。适用于正文已经基本成立，但用户说“开头留不住人”“标题描述第一句怎么配”“三处不要重复”“帮我做几组首屏方案”“正文有价值但点不开/看不下去”。从全文选择互补信息，比较人群基础与印象组合，生成多套组合并检查正文能否兑现。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-first-screen))
- **efficient-expression-flow**: 诊断并重写短视频或口播逐字稿的结构、段落衔接、信息密度、逐句节奏、情绪续航与口播流畅度。用户说“顺一下稿”“哪里会划走”“逻辑断了没有”“这段太啰嗦”“帮我压缩口播”“给我带标记修改稿和终稿”时使用，也适用于 DOCX、TXT、Markdown 中的长稿。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-flow))
- **efficient-expression-interview**: 为创始人、专家 IP、客户或案例对象设计并执行非诱导式对聊采访。围绕三张纸的空白格生成问题树、维度提示、案例召回问题、追问与停止规则、拍摄分段提示，并把回答整理成项目事实、内容线索和可用片段。用户说“明天采访怎么问”“帮我设计拍摄提纲”“怎么把案例问出来”“陪我做对聊”“这段访谈还缺什么”时使用。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-interview))
- **efficient-expression-practice**: 用刻意练习帮助创作者本人掌握高效表达，而不是代写。一次只训练一个判断动作，例如完整意思切分、逐句竞争、结构识别、并列排序、话题全貌、人群基础、印象组合、前三判断点或人产案选择；先让用户作答，再给证据化反馈和复练。用户说“带我练”“不要替我写”“我想自己学会拆稿/顺稿/转化”“给我出题并点评”时使用。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-practice))
- **efficient-expression-wording**: 优化一个完整意思、一句话或短段落的表达，不重构全文。根据句子功能、目标人群基础、印象组合和希望触发的感受，生成保守、平衡、强表达等不同版本，并解释各版适用人群、准确度和代价。用户说“这句话没感觉”“只改这一句”“说得更大众/更精准”“这个词怎么换”“不要改变事实和语气”时使用。 ([izhaohui2025/efficient-expression](https://github.com/izhaohui2025/efficient-expression/tree/HEAD/skills/efficient-expression-wording))

([fzfclee/consulting-skills](https://github.com/fzfclee/consulting-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effort-impact-matrix) · ★5)
- **effort-lifecycle**: This skill should be used when the user asks "what is an effort", "effort phases", "how do efforts work", "effort vs slice", or needs to understand the effort lifecycle. Covers the five phases: initialize, story capture, requirements, slice identification, and slice spawning. ([flox/forge-plugin](https://github.com/flox/forge-plugin/tree/HEAD/forge/skills/effort-lifecycle) · ★2)
- **effort-lifecycle**: Use at the START of any effort, before the first commit lands, to create its own isolated working copy, and again at the END to merge, push and tear that copy down. Covers naming it, resetting it onto the branch the work actually targets, committing from it, the green bar before a merge, and the teardown that is not optional. ([gnydick/ai-skills](https://github.com/gnydick/ai-skills/tree/HEAD/combine-projects-machinery/union/plugin/skills/effort-lifecycle))
- **effort-optimizer**: Optimize API costs by adjusting thinking effort per task type. Use with ralph-agent-teams or ralph-loop to reduce token usage on repetitive tasks while maximizing quality on critical tasks. Categorizes tasks (database, types, api, frontend, tests) and suggests effort levels (high/medium/low). ([JimmyBlanquet/project-forge](https://github.com/JimmyBlanquet/project-forge/tree/HEAD/skills/effort-optimizer))
- **effort-reduction**: Reduce friction and effort in an offer to increase its perceived value. Use when users feel overwhelmed, drop-off is high, or the offer feels too complex. Maps friction points, removes unnecessary steps, adds templates, and suggests automation and done-for-you elements. ([alexsmedile/hormozi-skills](https://github.com/alexsmedile/hormozi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effort-reduction) · ★171)
- **effort-router**: Pick and re-evaluate the output_config.effort level for a Claude Fable 5 workload. Use when designing a pipeline that mixes routine and hard tasks, when a call feels slow or expensive for what it's doing, or when porting an Opus 4.7/4.8 harness that defaulted to xhigh. ([kenhuangus/fable5](https://github.com/kenhuangus/fable5/tree/HEAD/skills/effort-router) · ★18)
- **effort-router**: Dado el nombre de la fase SDD actual, recomienda effort (low/medium/high/max) y modelo (Haiku/Sonnet/Opus) para cada agente. Evita pagar Opus cuando Sonnet o Haiku son suficientes — ahorro típico del 60-75% en fases mecánicas. ([carlos060798/FORGE](https://github.com/carlos060798/FORGE/tree/HEAD/skills/effort-router) · ★17)
- **effort-router**: タスクの規模・不確実性・可逆性・反復性を判定し、最適なeffortレベル/モデル/fast/プランモード/dynamic workflowを推奨してトークンを節約する。タスク着手前の見積もり、または「このタスクはどのeffortでやるべきか」と問われたときに使う ([fussy113/fussy113-agent-plugins](https://github.com/fussy113/fussy113-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/token-ops/skills/effort-router) · ★1)
- **effort-sizing**: Thin gating skill that classifies a task's complexity when the caller's own heuristics cannot confidently route it. Invoke when you need to decide between going straight to detailed planning versus running a design/ideation pass first, or when a task might be too large to plan directly. Trigger phrases include "score this task's complexity," "run the complexity gate," "decide writing-plans vs brainstorming," "is this task big enough to need a design phase," and "check if this needs to be split up." Called by sdlc-engine as a standing step, and by sdlc-brief or sdlc-direct only as a fallback w… ([Jarroslav/agentic-os](https://github.com/Jarroslav/agentic-os/tree/HEAD/plugins/agentic-sdlc/skills/effort-sizing))
- **effort-tuner**: Analyzes the current task and recommends the optimal Claude Code effort level (low | medium | high | xhigh | max) based on complexity heuristics. Helps avoid burning xhigh/max on trivial tasks or shipping undercooked analysis at low/medium. Use when: "effort tuner", "what effort", "effort level", "which effort should I use", "effort recommendation". ([claude-hangar/claude-hangar](https://github.com/claude-hangar/claude-hangar/tree/HEAD/core/skills/effort-tuner) · ★1)
- **effort-videos**: Use when creating or editing a Remotion video for Effort — the marketing hero, a feature/tutorial walkthrough, or any new composition. Covers the shared design system under remotion/ (tokens, fonts, VideoFrame, the component kit), brand + motion conventions, fps/resolution/duration norms, how real theme components star in videos, registration in root.tsx + the catalog, the player embeds on the landing/tutorials pages, and the render/preview commands. Read before touching anything under remotion/ or the tutorial player components. ([mdugue/activity-card](https://github.com/mdugue/activity-card/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/effort-videos))
- **effort-xhigh-pass**: Validate a skill when asked. Don't use for unrelated docs. ([luongnv89/asm](https://github.com/luongnv89/asm/tree/HEAD/src/eval/providers/skill-best-practice/v1/fixtures/effort-xhigh-pass) · ★936)

Airtable-connected ERB projects only; scalar fields and CRUD go through effortless-airtable instead. - **effortless-bases**: Spin up, credential, apply schema to, and secure (magic-links + RLS) a Postgres base on bases.effortlessapi.com. ([EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills](https://github.com/EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effortless-bases) · ★1)

Triggers: "bootstrap an effortless project from this text", "shadle steps". Not the CLI binary (that is effortless-cli). ([EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills](https://github.com/EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effortless-claude-updates) · ★1)
- **effortless-cli**: The `effortless` CLI (aka ssotme/aicapture/aic) — install/update the binary and use its commands: `effortless: command not found`, login, `-init`, `build`, `-setAccountAPIKey`, `-install &lt;transpiler&gt;`, build flags, project file structure. Not the skill set (that is effortless-claude-updates). ([EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills](https://github.com/EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effortless-cli) · ★1)
- **effortless-cmcc**: Load BEFORE answering any evaluative or "why" question about Effortless/ERB — "what do you make of this", "is this any good", "could this work for X", "isn't this overkill", "is the rulebook sufficient", or any mention of CMCC, SDLAF, the 5 primitives, bitemporal ACID DAG. - **effortless-conventions**: ERB naming and DAG rules — PascalCase plural tables, the Name field, PK/FK patterns, singular FK / plural reverse FK, why many-to-many is not allowed. ERB projects only. ([EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills](https://github.com/EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effortless-conventions) · ★1)
- **effortless-demo-app**: Spin up a complete Effortless POC from a one-line domain description — Express + Vite SPA over a Postgres-generated rulebook with a multi-hop calculated-field DAG. - **effortless-diagnostics**: ERB project health — DAG integrity, broken FK targets, JOIN anti-patterns in app code, migrating legacy base-table reads to vw_* views, diagnostic queries. - **effortless-loop**: The iterative ERB cycle CHANGE-RULE → REBUILD → CONSUME-VIEWS. - **effortless-meta-table**: The `__meta__` table — a transpiler-ignored, global name/value settings entity for parameters, thresholds, choices and phrase atoms that belong to no domain entity (not the `_meta` section). Triggers: "add a __meta__ table", "global settings table", "where do I put global parameters". ERB projects only. - **effortless-orchestrator**: Top-level entry point for Effortless Rulebook (ERB) projects — any project with effortless.json or an effortless-rulebook/ directory, or any request to set up / install / update Effortless tooling. Holds the mental model, axioms, guardrails, token discipline, the load-gate policy for every effortless-* skill, and the routing table. Load this first. ([EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills](https://github.com/EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effortless-orchestrator) · ★1)
- **effortless-pipeline**: The ERB build pipeline — effortless.json, ProjectTranspilers, the transpiler catalog, `effortless build` vs `-id`, transpiler install paths, how the build flows from the rulebook hub to generated code. ERB projects only. ([EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills](https://github.com/EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effortless-pipeline) · ★1)
- **effortless-progress-report**: Delivery / status / priced-plan reports derived from the rulebook via rulebook-to-progress-report; also adds the delivery spine (ERBUserStories, ERBAcceptanceCriteria, ERBBuildPhases, ERBEffortClasses) to a rulebook that lacks one — via the editor's Modules → Delivery, or by hand. - **effortless-publish-tool**: Publish / push / deploy / release a new version of a transpiler tool in Versioned-Stable-SSoTme-Tools — the one scripted path (scripts/publish-tool.sh) mirroring the 🚀 Deploy button. Triggers: "publish rulebook-to-X", "ship this transpiler", "make the new version live". Not `effortless build`, not build-and-push-cpln-workload.sh alone. Requires that repo. Pair with effortless-cmcc (theory) and effortless-rulebooks (proof). ([EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills](https://github.com/EffortlessAPI/effortless-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/effortless-rationale) · ★1)

Triggers: "set up rulebook dev-ops", "deployment management page", "promote a release", "cut a version", "migration matrix", "push code to production". Heavy; invoke once per project. ARM64 + COBOL), StarTrek demo, and the github.com/effortlessapi/effortless-rulebooks repo. Triggers: "show me the receipts", "does this run in code", "what's in the rulebooks repo". - **effortless-seeds**: Effortless seeds — starting a project from a public GitHub repository and publishing one: "list seeds", "clone a seed", "start from a seed", "make this repo a seed", "seed sources", "effortless seed", `listSeeds`, `cloneSeed`, `addSeedSource`, `effortless-seed.json`, `$key$` replacements. - **effortlist-ai**: Manage EffortList AI folders, tasks, and todos. Use when the user wants to organize their life, track projects, or manage schedules via the EffortList AI platform. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/effortlist-ai) · ★15)
- **effortmine**: Decompose a request into subtasks, classify each by difficulty (T1 mechanical through T4 hard reasoning, plus R-research and C-coding), and dispatch each to the cheapest calibrated reasoning-effort tier a blind grader still accepts. Use when you want a multi-part job done at the right effort per part instead of burning one uniform (usually too-high) effort on everything. The per-subtask effort is applied by dispatching to a tier-pinned worker agent (miner-low through miner-max), because Claude Code has no per-spawn effort parameter. ([nagisanzenin/effortmining](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/effortmining/tree/HEAD/skills/effortmine) · ★31)

- **efirm-client-intake-form**: Use when a law firm needs to open a new client file — the skill generates a tailored intake form, captures identity and entity information, collects opposing-party details for conflict checking, triggers AML/sanctions screening, and fires the downstream engagement workflow. - **ein-llc-activation-continuation**: Acquire the public EIN.LLC activation continuation 0.1.5 archive and continue one caller-supplied business context with discover, assess, prepare, resume, status, show, or cancel.
